- [ ] **Step 4: Write `internal/couch/bulk.go`**

```go
package couch

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"net/url"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/path"
)

// ChangeRow is one entry of the changes feed.
type ChangeRow struct {
	ID      string
	Seq     string
	Deleted bool
	// Revs holds every leaf revision, because the feed is read with
	// style=all_docs so conflicts are not lost.
	Revs []string
}

// ChangesPage is one batch of the changes feed.
type ChangesPage struct {
	Rows    []ChangeRow
	LastSeq string
	Pending int64
}

// Changes reads one batch of the normal changes feed with style=all_docs.
//
// CouchDB does not honour revs=true on _changes (verified on 3.5.2), so this
// returns ids and leaf revisions only; use BulkGet to fetch full documents
// including _revisions.
func (c *Client) Changes(ctx context.Context, db, since string, limit int) (ChangesPage, error) {
	q := url.Values{}
	q.Set("feed", "normal")
	q.Set("style", "all_docs")
	if since == "" {
		since = "0"
	}
	q.Set("since", since)
	if limit > 0 {
		q.Set("limit", strconv.Itoa(limit))
	}
	var body struct {
		Results []struct {
			Seq     json.RawMessage `json:"seq"`
			ID      string          `json:"id"`
			Deleted bool            `json:"deleted"`
			Changes []struct {
				Rev string `json:"rev"`
			} `json:"changes"`
		} `json:"results"`
		LastSeq json.RawMessage `json:"last_seq"`
		Pending int64           `json:"pending"`
	}
	apiPath := "/" + path.Encode(db) + "/_changes?" + q.Encode()
	if err := c.DoJSON(ctx, "GET", apiPath, nil, &body, "read", fmt.Sprintf("changes for %q", db)); err != nil {
		return ChangesPage{}, err
	}
	page := ChangesPage{LastSeq: seqString(body.LastSeq), Pending: body.Pending}
	for _, r := range body.Results {
		row := ChangeRow{ID: r.ID, Seq: seqString(r.Seq), Deleted: r.Deleted}
		for _, ch := range r.Changes {
			row.Revs = append(row.Revs, ch.Rev)
		}
		page.Rows = append(page.Rows, row)
	}
	return page, nil
}

// seqString renders a sequence, which CouchDB 3.x sends as a string but older
// or clustered responses may send as a number.
func seqString(raw json.RawMessage) string {
	if len(raw) == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	var s string
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &s); err == nil {
		return s
	}
	return string(raw)
}

// BulkRef names one document revision for BulkGet.
type BulkRef struct {
	ID  string `json:"id"`
	Rev string `json:"rev,omitempty"`
}

// BulkGet fetches full documents. With revs set, each document carries its
// _revisions history, which restore needs for new_edits=false. Entries the
// server reports as errors are skipped.
func (c *Client) BulkGet(ctx context.Context, db string, refs []BulkRef, revs bool) ([]json.RawMessage, error) {
	if len(refs) == 0 {
		return nil, nil
	}
	apiPath := "/" + path.Encode(db) + "/_bulk_get"
	if revs {
		apiPath += "?revs=true"
	}
	var body struct {
		Results []struct {
			ID   string `json:"id"`
			Docs []struct {
				OK    json.RawMessage `json:"ok"`
				Error json.RawMessage `json:"error"`
			} `json:"docs"`
		} `json:"results"`
	}
	req := map[string]any{"docs": refs}
	if err := c.DoJSON(ctx, "POST", apiPath, req, &body, "read", fmt.Sprintf("documents in %q", db)); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out []json.RawMessage
	for _, r := range body.Results {
		for _, d := range r.Docs {
			if len(d.OK) > 0 {
				out = append(out, d.OK)
			}
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// BulkError is one per-document failure from _bulk_docs.
type BulkError struct {
	ID     string `json:"id"`
	Rev    string `json:"rev"`
	Error  string `json:"error"`
	Reason string `json:"reason"`
}

// BulkDocs writes a batch. With newEdits false, CouchDB keeps each document's
// supplied _rev and _revisions, which is how restore preserves revisions;
// documents must not carry attachment stubs in that mode.
func (c *Client) BulkDocs(ctx context.Context, db string, docs []json.RawMessage, newEdits bool) ([]BulkError, error) {
	if len(docs) == 0 {
		return nil, nil
	}
	req := map[string]any{"docs": docs}
	if !newEdits {
		req["new_edits"] = false
	}
	var results []BulkError
	apiPath := "/" + path.Encode(db) + "/_bulk_docs"
	if err := c.DoJSON(ctx, "POST", apiPath, req, &results, "write", fmt.Sprintf("documents in %q", db)); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var failures []BulkError
	for _, r := range results {
		if r.Error != "" {
			failures = append(failures, r)
		}
	}
	return failures, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `internal/command/backup.go`**

```go
package command

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"sort"
	"time"

	"github.com/spf13/pflag"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/backup"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/couch"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/path"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/session"
)

// backupBatchSize is how many changes are read, and documents fetched, per
// checkpoint.
const backupBatchSize = 200

// Backup returns the backup command.
func Backup() Command {
	return Command{
		Name:        "backup",
		Summary:     "Write a database, with attachments, to a dump file",
		Usage:       "<db-path> <file>",
		MinArgs:     2,
		MaxArgs:     2,
		NeedsClient: true,
		Complete:    completePath,
		Flags: func(fs *pflag.FlagSet) {
			fs.Bool("resume", false, "continue an interrupted dump")
			fs.String("since", "", "start from this update sequence")
			fs.Int("batch", backupBatchSize, "documents per checkpoint")
		},
		Run: func(ctx context.Context, s *session.Session, inv Invocation) (Result, error) {
			t, err := s.Resolve(inv.Arg(0))
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			if t.Kind != path.KindDatabase {
				return nil, Usagef("backup", "%s is a %s; backup takes a database path", t.Path, t.Kind)
			}
			file := inv.Arg(1)
			batch := inv.Int("batch")
			if batch <= 0 {
				batch = backupBatchSize
			}

			since := inv.String("since")
			var docs, atts, written int64
			var f *os.File

			if inv.Bool("resume") {
				f, err = os.OpenFile(file, os.O_RDWR, 0o600)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				res, serr := backup.Scan(f)
				if serr != nil {
					f.Close()
					return nil, serr
				}
				if res.Complete {
					f.Close()
					return Message{Text: fmt.Sprintf("%s already contains a complete dump. Nothing to resume.", file)}, nil
				}
				f.Close()
				if err := backup.Truncate(file, res.Offset); err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				f, err = os.OpenFile(file, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_APPEND, 0o600)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				if since == "" {
					since = res.Seq
				}
				docs, atts, written = res.Docs, res.Attachments, res.Bytes
				fmt.Fprintf(s.Stderr, "Resuming %s from sequence %s (%d documents, %s already written).\n", file, since, docs, humanBytes(written))
			} else {
				f, err = os.OpenFile(file, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
				if err != nil {
					if os.IsExist(err) {
						return nil, Usagef("backup", "%s already exists; pass --resume to continue it, or choose another file", file)
					}
					return nil, err
				}
			}
			defer f.Close()

			w := backup.NewWriter(f)
			if docs == 0 && atts == 0 && since == "" {
				info, err := s.Client.DatabaseInfo(ctx, t.Database)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				server, err := s.Client.ServerInfo(ctx)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				if err := w.WriteHeader(backup.Header{
					DB:          t.Database,
					Server:      server.Version,
					Started:     time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
					Partitioned: info.Partitioned,
				}); err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
			}

			lastSeq := since
			for {
				page, err := s.Client.Changes(ctx, t.Database, lastSeq, batch)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				if len(page.Rows) == 0 {
					lastSeq = page.LastSeq
					break
				}
				refs := make([]couch.BulkRef, 0, len(page.Rows))
				for _, row := range page.Rows {
					if row.Deleted {
						continue
					}
					for _, rev := range row.Revs {
						refs = append(refs, couch.BulkRef{ID: row.ID, Rev: rev})
					}
				}
				bodies, err := s.Client.BulkGet(ctx, t.Database, refs, true)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				for _, body := range bodies {
					stripped, attachments, id, rev, err := splitAttachments(body)
					if err != nil {
						return nil, err
					}
					if err := w.WriteDoc(stripped); err != nil {
						return nil, err
					}
					docs++
					written += int64(len(stripped))
					for _, name := range attachments {
						n, err := writeAttachment(ctx, s, w, t.Database, id, rev, name)
						if err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
						atts++
						written += n
					}
				}
				lastSeq = page.LastSeq
				if err := w.WriteCheckpoint(lastSeq); err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				// Spec section 9: progress carries document and byte counts.
				fmt.Fprintf(s.Stderr, "\r%d documents, %d attachments, %s…", docs, atts, humanBytes(written))
				if page.Pending == 0 && len(page.Rows) < batch {
					break
				}
			}
			fmt.Fprintln(s.Stderr)

			if err := w.WriteFooter(backup.Footer{Docs: docs, Attachments: atts, LastSeq: lastSeq}); err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			if err := w.Close(); err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			return Message{Text: fmt.Sprintf("Wrote %d document(s), %d attachment(s) and %s from %q to %s (sequence %s).", docs, atts, humanBytes(written), t.Database, file, lastSeq)}, nil
		},
	}
}

// splitAttachments removes the _attachments stubs from a document, because
// _bulk_docs with new_edits=false rejects them, and returns their names along
// with the document id and revision.
func splitAttachments(body json.RawMessage) (json.RawMessage, []string, string, string, error) {
	var m map[string]json.RawMessage
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &m); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, "", "", err
	}
	var id, rev string
	_ = json.Unmarshal(m["_id"], &id)
	_ = json.Unmarshal(m["_rev"], &rev)

	var names []string
	if raw, ok := m["_attachments"]; ok {
		var atts map[string]json.RawMessage
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &atts); err != nil {
			return nil, nil, "", "", err
		}
		for name := range atts {
			names = append(names, name)
		}
		delete(m, "_attachments")
	}
	sort.Strings(names)
	stripped, err := json.Marshal(m)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, "", "", err
	}
	return stripped, names, id, rev, nil
}

// writeAttachment spills an attachment to a temp file to learn its exact size,
// then writes the att record and copies the bytes into the dump. It returns the
// number of payload bytes written, for the progress counter.
//
// CouchDB's reported attachment length is the gzip-compressed size when it
// stored the attachment compressed, and a streamed GET has no Content-Length,
// so the byte count has to be measured locally.
func writeAttachment(ctx context.Context, s *session.Session, w *backup.Writer, db, id, rev, name string) (int64, error) {
	att, err := s.Client.GetAttachment(ctx, db, id, name, rev)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	defer att.Content.Close()

	spill, err := os.CreateTemp("", "cdb-att")
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	defer func() {
		spill.Close()
		os.Remove(spill.Name())
	}()

	n, err := io.Copy(spill, att.Content)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	if _, err := spill.Seek(0, io.SeekStart); err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	if err := w.WriteAttachment(backup.Att{
		ID:          id,
		Rev:         rev,
		Name:        name,
		ContentType: att.ContentType,
		Length:      n,
	}, spill); err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	return n, nil
}
```

### Task 19: Restore

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/command/restore.go`
- Modify: `internal/command/registry_default.go`
- Test: `internal/command/restore_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `backup.NewReader/Reader.Next/Record/Header/Att/Footer/KindHeader/KindDoc/KindAtt/KindCheckpoint/KindFooter`, `couch.Client.BulkDocs/CreateDatabase/DatabaseInfo/DatabaseExists/PutAttachment`, `command.Message/Usagef/Confirm`, `path.KindDatabase`.
- Produces:
  ```go
  package command
  func Restore() Command
  ```

**Verified restore behaviour** (CouchDB 3.5.2):
- `_bulk_docs` with `new_edits=false` **rejects** a document that still carries
  `"_attachments": {"x": {"stub": true, …}}` with
  `412 Invalid attachment stub`. Backup already strips them (Task 18).
- Inlining the attachment as base64 in the same `_bulk_docs new_edits=false`
  body restores the document at its **original revision** and stores the
  attachment content. This is the default path here, for attachments up to
  `inlineAttachmentLimit`.
- Uploading the attachment separately with `PUT` afterwards **bumps** the
  revision (`2-6707…` becomes `3-bb56…`). That path is used only for
  attachments larger than the limit, and the command prints which documents
  changed revision.

```go
package command

import (

)

// writeDump builds a small dump file and returns its path.
func writeDump(t *testing.T, partitioned bool) string {
	t.Helper()
	name := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "dump.cdb.gz")
	f, err := os.Create(name)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer f.Close()
	w := backup.NewWriter(f)
	if err := w.WriteHeader(backup.Header{DB: "mydb", Server: "3.5.2", Partitioned: partitioned}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.WriteDoc(json.RawMessage(`{"_id":"a","_rev":"1-aa","_revisions":{"start":1,"ids":["aa"]},"v":1}`)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.WriteDoc(json.RawMessage(`{"_id":"b","_rev":"2-bb","_revisions":{"start":2,"ids":["bb","cc"]},"v":2}`)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.WriteAttachment(backup.Att{ID: "b", Rev: "2-bb", Name: "note.txt", ContentType: "text/plain", Length: 5}, strings.NewReader("hello")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.WriteCheckpoint("2-y"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.WriteFooter(backup.Footer{Docs: 2, Attachments: 1, LastSeq: "2-y"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return name
}

func TestRestoreIntoAnEmptyDatabaseInlinesAttachments(t *testing.T) {
	srv := couchtest.New(t)
	srv.JSON("GET", "/target", 200, `{"db_name":"target","doc_count":0,"sizes":{"file":1,"external":1},"cluster":{"q":1,"n":1},"props":{}}`)
	srv.On("HEAD", "/target", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) { w.WriteHeader(200) })
	srv.JSON("POST", "/target/_bulk_docs", 201, `[]`)
	dump := writeDump(t, false)
	s := connected(t, srv)

	res, err := invoke(t, Restore(), s, dump, "/target")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, ok := res.(Message); !ok {
		t.Fatalf("result is %T, want Message", res)
	}
	body := string(srv.Last("POST", "/target/_bulk_docs").Body)
	if !strings.Contains(body, `"new_edits":false`) {
		t.Errorf("bulk body = %s, want new_edits false", body)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(body, `"_revisions"`) {
		t.Errorf("bulk body = %s, want _revisions preserved", body)
	}
	// "hello" base64-encoded.
	if !strings.Contains(body, `"aGVsbG8="`) {
		t.Errorf("bulk body = %s, want the attachment inlined as base64", body)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(body, `"note.txt"`) {
		t.Errorf("bulk body = %s, want the attachment name", body)
	}
	if strings.Contains(body, `"stub":true`) {
		t.Errorf("bulk body = %s, must not contain an attachment stub", body)
	}
	if srv.Last("PUT", "/target/b/note.txt") != nil {
		t.Error("restore uploaded the attachment separately, which bumps the revision")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `internal/command/restore.go`**

```go
package command

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"strings"

	"github.com/spf13/pflag"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/backup"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/path"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/session"
)

// inlineAttachmentLimit is the largest attachment restored inline as base64
// inside the _bulk_docs body. Inlining preserves the original revision;
// anything larger is uploaded separately, which bumps the revision.
const inlineAttachmentLimit = 4 << 20 // 4 MiB

// restoreBatchSize is how many documents are written per _bulk_docs call.
const restoreBatchSize = 200

// pendingDoc is a document waiting to be written, with any attachments that
// arrived with it.
type pendingDoc struct {
	id     string
	rev    string
	fields map[string]json.RawMessage
	inline map[string]json.RawMessage
	large  []largeAttachment
}

// largeAttachment is an attachment spilled to a temp file because it exceeds
// inlineAttachmentLimit.
type largeAttachment struct {
	name        string
	contentType string
	size        int64
	file        string
}

// Restore returns the restore command.
func Restore() Command {
	return Command{
		Name:        "restore",
		Summary:     "Load a dump file into a database",
		Usage:       "<file> <db-path>",
		MinArgs:     2,
		MaxArgs:     2,
		NeedsClient: true,
		Complete:    completePath,
		Flags: func(fs *pflag.FlagSet) {
			fs.Bool("create", false, "create the target database if it does not exist")
			fs.Bool("merge", false, "allow restoring into a database that already has documents")
			fs.Int("batch", restoreBatchSize, "documents per bulk write")
		},
		Run: func(ctx context.Context, s *session.Session, inv Invocation) (Result, error) {
			file := inv.Arg(0)
			t, err := s.Resolve(inv.Arg(1))
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			if t.Kind != path.KindDatabase {
				return nil, Usagef("restore", "%s is a %s; restore takes a database path", t.Path, t.Kind)
			}
			batch := inv.Int("batch")
			if batch <= 0 {
				batch = restoreBatchSize
			}

			f, err := os.Open(file)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			defer f.Close()
			r, err := backup.NewReader(f)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			defer r.Close()

			var (
				header              *backup.Header
				prepared            bool
				pending             []*pendingDoc
				current             *pendingDoc
				docs, atts, written int64
				bumped              []string
				tempFiles           []string
			)
			defer func() {
				for _, p := range tempFiles {
					os.Remove(p)
				}
			}()

			flush := func() error {
				if len(pending) == 0 {
					return nil
				}
				bodies := make([]json.RawMessage, 0, len(pending))
				for _, p := range pending {
					body, err := p.marshal()
					if err != nil {
						return err
					}
					bodies = append(bodies, body)
				}
				failures, err := s.Client.BulkDocs(ctx, t.Database, bodies, false)
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}
				if len(failures) > 0 {
					var msgs []string
					for _, fl := range failures {
						msgs = append(msgs, fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s (%s)", fl.ID, fl.Error, fl.Reason))
					}
					return fmt.Errorf("the server rejected %d document(s): %s", len(failures), strings.Join(msgs, "; "))
				}
				for _, p := range pending {
					for _, la := range p.large {
						af, err := os.Open(la.file)
						if err != nil {
							return err
						}
						newRev, err := s.Client.PutAttachment(ctx, t.Database, p.id, la.name, la.contentType, la.size, af, p.rev)
						af.Close()
						if err != nil {
							return err
						}
						bumped = append(bumped, fmt.Sprintf("%s (%s -> %s)", p.id, p.rev, newRev))
						p.rev = newRev
					}
				}
				pending = pending[:0]
				// Spec section 9: progress carries document and byte counts.
				fmt.Fprintf(s.Stderr, "\r%d documents, %d attachments, %s…", docs, atts, humanBytes(written))
				return nil
			}

			for {
				rec, err := r.Next()
				if err == io.EOF {
					break
				}
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				switch rec.Kind {
				case backup.KindHeader:
					header = rec.Header

				case backup.KindDoc:
					if !prepared {
						if err := prepareTarget(ctx, s, t, header, inv.Bool("create"), inv.Bool("merge")); err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
						prepared = true
					}
					p, err := newPendingDoc(rec.Doc)
					if err != nil {
						return nil, err
					}
					pending = append(pending, p)
					current = p
					docs++
					written += int64(len(rec.Doc))
					if len(pending) >= batch {
						if err := flush(); err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
						current = nil
					}

				case backup.KindAtt:
					if current == nil || current.id != rec.Att.ID {
						// The attachment belongs to a document already written,
						// so upload it after the fact.
						name, err := spillAttachment(rec.Content, rec.Att.Length)
						if err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
						tempFiles = append(tempFiles, name)
						af, err := os.Open(name)
						if err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
						newRev, err := s.Client.PutAttachment(ctx, t.Database, rec.Att.ID, rec.Att.Name, rec.Att.ContentType, rec.Att.Length, af, rec.Att.Rev)
						af.Close()
						if err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
						bumped = append(bumped, fmt.Sprintf("%s (%s -> %s)", rec.Att.ID, rec.Att.Rev, newRev))
						atts++
						written += rec.Att.Length
						continue
					}
					if rec.Att.Length <= inlineAttachmentLimit {
						data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(rec.Content, rec.Att.Length))
						if err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
						entry, err := json.Marshal(map[string]string{
							"content_type": rec.Att.ContentType,
							"data":         base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(data),
						})
						if err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
						if current.inline == nil {
							current.inline = map[string]json.RawMessage{}
						}
						current.inline[rec.Att.Name] = entry
					} else {
						name, err := spillAttachment(rec.Content, rec.Att.Length)
						if err != nil {
							return nil, err
						}
						tempFiles = append(tempFiles, name)
						current.large = append(current.large, largeAttachment{
							name:        rec.Att.Name,
							contentType: rec.Att.ContentType,
							size:        rec.Att.Length,
							file:        name,
						})
					}
					atts++
					written += rec.Att.Length

				case backup.KindCheckpoint:
					if err := flush(); err != nil {
						return nil, err
					}
					current = nil
				}
			}
			if !prepared {
				// An empty dump still has to create the target when --create
				// was passed, and still has to refuse a non-empty one.
				if err := prepareTarget(ctx, s, t, header, inv.Bool("create"), inv.Bool("merge")); err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				prepared = true
			}
			if err := flush(); err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			fmt.Fprintln(s.Stderr)

			text := fmt.Sprintf("Restored %d document(s), %d attachment(s) and %s into %q.", docs, atts, humanBytes(written), t.Database)
			if len(bumped) > 0 {
				text += fmt.Sprintf("\n%d document(s) changed revision because their attachments were too large to inline: %s", len(bumped), strings.Join(bumped, ", "))
			}
			return Message{Text: text}, nil
		},
	}
}

// prepareTarget creates or validates the destination database.
func prepareTarget(ctx context.Context, s *session.Session, t path.Target, header *backup.Header, create, merge bool) error {
	exists, err := s.Client.DatabaseExists(ctx, t.Database)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if !exists {
		if !create {
			return Usagef("restore", "database %q does not exist; pass --create to create it", t.Database)
		}
		partitioned := header != nil && header.Partitioned
		return s.Client.CreateDatabase(ctx, t.Database, partitioned, 0)
	}
	info, err := s.Client.DatabaseInfo(ctx, t.Database)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if info.DocCount > 0 && !merge {
		return Usagef("restore", "database %q already holds %d document(s); pass --merge to restore into it anyway", t.Database, info.DocCount)
	}
	return nil
}

// newPendingDoc decodes a dump document for later writing.
func newPendingDoc(body json.RawMessage) (*pendingDoc, error) {
	var m map[string]json.RawMessage
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &m); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	p := &pendingDoc{fields: m}
	_ = json.Unmarshal(m["_id"], &p.id)
	_ = json.Unmarshal(m["_rev"], &p.rev)
	return p, nil
}

// marshal renders the document, inlining any small attachments so that
// _bulk_docs with new_edits=false keeps the original revision.
func (p *pendingDoc) marshal() (json.RawMessage, error) {
	if len(p.inline) > 0 {
		atts, err := json.Marshal(p.inline)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		p.fields["_attachments"] = atts
	}
	return json.Marshal(p.fields)
}

// spillAttachment copies exactly length bytes into a temp file and returns its
// path. The caller removes the file.
func spillAttachment(r io.Reader, length int64) (string, error) {
	f, err := os.CreateTemp("", "cdb-restore")
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	n, err := io.Copy(f, io.LimitReader(r, length))
	if err != nil {
		os.Remove(f.Name())
		return "", err
	}
	if n != length {
		os.Remove(f.Name())
		return "", fmt.Errorf("attachment payload was %d bytes, expected %d", n, length)
	}
	return f.Name(), nil
}
```

Note: inlining attachments requires the multipart PUT path to stay off, and
`BulkDocs` posts a plain JSON body through `Client.DoJSON`, so Kivik's
multipart behaviour is not involved at all. That is exactly the code path
verified to preserve the original revision.

- [ ] **Step 6: Verify a full round trip against Docker**

```bash
go build -o /tmp/cdb ./cmd/cdb
export CDB_URL=http://localhost:15984 CDB_USER=admin CDB_PASSWORD=password
rm -f /tmp/rt.cdb.gz
/tmp/cdb backup /lstest /tmp/rt.cdb.gz
/tmp/cdb restore /tmp/rt.cdb.gz /lstest_copy --create

curl -s -u admin:password "$CDB_URL/lstest/holder" | python3 -c 'import sys,json;print(json.load(sys.stdin)["_rev"])'
curl -s -u admin:password "$CDB_URL/lstest_copy/holder" | python3 -c 'import sys,json;print(json.load(sys.stdin)["_rev"])'
/tmp/cdb fetch /lstest_copy/holder/blob.bin /tmp/blob.copy
cmp /tmp/blob.bin /tmp/blob.copy && echo "attachment survived the round trip"
```
Expected: both databases report the same document count, `holder` has the
**same revision** in both (the attachment is under 4 MiB so it was inlined),
and the attachment bytes match.

### Task 20: Replication — `replicate` and `replications`

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/replicate/replicate.go`
- Create: `internal/command/replicate.go`
- Modify: `internal/command/database.go` (`copyDatabase` calls `replicate.Create`)
- Modify: `internal/command/registry_default.go`
- Test: `internal/replicate/replicate_test.go`
- Test: `internal/command/replicate_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `couch.Client.DoJSON/URL/GetRev/DeleteDocument`, `path.Resolve/Encode/Target`, `session.Session`, `command.Rows/Row/Column/Message/Stream/Confirm/Usagef`.
- Produces:
  ```go
  package replicate
  type Request struct {
      ID           string
      Source       string // full URL
      Target       string // full URL
      Continuous   bool
      CreateTarget bool
      Filter       string
  }
  // Marshalled straight into command.Row.JSON, so every field carries a
  // lower-case json tag like the rest of the --json output.
  type Status struct {
      DocID       string          `json:"doc_id"`
      ID          string          `json:"id"`
      Source      string          `json:"source"`
      Target      string          `json:"target"`
      State       string          `json:"state"`
      Node        string          `json:"node"`
      Error       string          `json:"error,omitempty"`
      ErrorCount  int64           `json:"error_count"`
      LastUpdated string          `json:"last_updated"`
      Info        json.RawMessage `json:"info,omitempty"`
  }
  func Create(ctx context.Context, cl *couch.Client, req Request) (string, error)
  func List(ctx context.Context, cl *couch.Client) ([]Status, error)
  func Show(ctx context.Context, cl *couch.Client, docID string) (Status, error)
  func Cancel(ctx context.Context, cl *couch.Client, docID string) error
  // Endpoint turns a virtual path into a full URL, or passes a URL through.
  func Endpoint(cl *couch.Client, base, s string) (string, error)

  package command
  func Replicate() Command
  func Replications() Command
  ```

```go
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got != "https://other.example.com/remote" {
		t.Errorf("Endpoint(url) = %q", got)
	}
	if _, err := Endpoint(c, "/", "/mydb/doc1"); err == nil {
		t.Error("Endpoint accepted a document path")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `internal/replicate/replicate.go`**

```go
// Package replicate writes _replicator documents and reads the replication
// scheduler.
package replicate

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"strings"

	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/couch"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/path"
)

// Request describes a replication to create.
type Request struct {
	ID           string
	Source       string
	Target       string
	Continuous   bool
	CreateTarget bool
	Filter       string
}

// Status is one entry of _scheduler/docs. It is only ever marshalled (the wire
// shape is schedulerDoc below), and it goes straight into command.Row.JSON, so
// the json tags are load-bearing: "replications --json" must emit lower-case
// keys like every other command.
type Status struct {
	DocID       string          `json:"doc_id"`
	ID          string          `json:"id"`
	Source      string          `json:"source"`
	Target      string          `json:"target"`
	State       string          `json:"state"`
	Node        string          `json:"node"`
	Error       string          `json:"error,omitempty"`
	ErrorCount  int64           `json:"error_count"`
	LastUpdated string          `json:"last_updated"`
	Info        json.RawMessage `json:"info,omitempty"`
}

// Create writes a document into _replicator and returns its id.
func Create(ctx context.Context, cl *couch.Client, req Request) (string, error) {
	doc := map[string]any{"source": req.Source, "target": req.Target}
	if req.ID != "" {
		doc["_id"] = req.ID
	}
	if req.Continuous {
		doc["continuous"] = true
	}
	if req.CreateTarget {
		doc["create_target"] = true
	}
	if req.Filter != "" {
		doc["filter"] = req.Filter
	}
	var out struct {
		ID string `json:"id"`
	}
	if err := cl.DoJSON(ctx, "POST", "/_replicator", doc, &out, "create", "replication"); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return out.ID, nil
}

// schedulerDoc is the wire shape of a _scheduler/docs entry.
type schedulerDoc struct {
	Database    string          `json:"database"`
	DocID       string          `json:"doc_id"`
	ID          *string         `json:"id"`
	Source      string          `json:"source"`
	Target      string          `json:"target"`
	State       string          `json:"state"`
	Node        string          `json:"node"`
	ErrorCount  int64           `json:"error_count"`
	LastUpdated string          `json:"last_updated"`
	Info        json.RawMessage `json:"info"`
}

func (d schedulerDoc) toStatus() Status {
	st := Status{
		DocID:       d.DocID,
		Source:      d.Source,
		Target:      d.Target,
		State:       d.State,
		Node:        d.Node,
		ErrorCount:  d.ErrorCount,
		LastUpdated: d.LastUpdated,
		Info:        d.Info,
	}
	if d.ID != nil {
		st.ID = *d.ID
	}
	if len(d.Info) > 0 {
		var info struct {
			Error string `json:"error"`
		}
		if err := json.Unmarshal(d.Info, &info); err == nil {
			st.Error = info.Error
		}
	}
	return st
}

// List reads every scheduler entry.
func List(ctx context.Context, cl *couch.Client) ([]Status, error) {
	var body struct {
		Docs []schedulerDoc `json:"docs"`
	}
	if err := cl.DoJSON(ctx, "GET", "/_scheduler/docs", nil, &body, "list", "replications"); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]Status, 0, len(body.Docs))
	for _, d := range body.Docs {
		out = append(out, d.toStatus())
	}
	return out, nil
}

// Show reads one scheduler entry by its _replicator document id.
func Show(ctx context.Context, cl *couch.Client, docID string) (Status, error) {
	var d schedulerDoc
	apiPath := "/_scheduler/docs/_replicator/" + path.Encode(docID)
	if err := cl.DoJSON(ctx, "GET", apiPath, nil, &d, "read", fmt.Sprintf("replication %q", docID)); err != nil {
		return Status{}, err
	}
	return d.toStatus(), nil
}

// Cancel deletes the _replicator document, which stops the job.
func Cancel(ctx context.Context, cl *couch.Client, docID string) error {
	rev, err := cl.GetRev(ctx, "_replicator", docID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, err = cl.DeleteDocument(ctx, "_replicator", docID, rev)
	return err
}

// Endpoint turns a virtual database path into a full URL against the connected
// server, and passes an http(s) URL through unchanged.
func Endpoint(cl *couch.Client, base, s string) (string, error) {
	if strings.HasPrefix(s, "http://") || strings.HasPrefix(s, "https://") {
		return s, nil
	}
	t, err := path.Resolve(base, s)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if t.Kind != path.KindDatabase {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%s is a %s; replication endpoints are databases or full URLs", t.Path, t.Kind)
	}
	return cl.URL() + "/" + path.Encode(t.Database), nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `internal/command/replicate.go`**

```go
package command

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/spf13/pflag"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/replicate"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/session"
)

// watchInterval is how often --watch redraws.
const watchInterval = 2 * time.Second

// Replicate returns the replicate command.
func Replicate() Command {
	return Command{
		Name:        "replicate",
		Summary:     "Start a replication between two databases",
		Usage:       "<source> <target>",
		MinArgs:     2,
		MaxArgs:     2,
		NeedsClient: true,
		Complete:    completePath,
		Flags: func(fs *pflag.FlagSet) {
			fs.Bool("continuous", false, "keep replicating as changes arrive")
			fs.Bool("create-target", false, "create the target database if it is missing")
			fs.String("filter", "", "design document filter, as ddoc/name")
			fs.String("id", "", "document id for the replication job")
		},
		Run: func(ctx context.Context, s *session.Session, inv Invocation) (Result, error) {
			source, err := replicate.Endpoint(s.Client, s.Path(), inv.Arg(0))
			if err != nil {
				return nil, Usagef("replicate", "%v", err)
			}
			target, err := replicate.Endpoint(s.Client, s.Path(), inv.Arg(1))
			if err != nil {
				return nil, Usagef("replicate", "%v", err)
			}
			id, err := replicate.Create(ctx, s.Client, replicate.Request{
				ID:           inv.String("id"),
				Source:       source,
				Target:       target,
				Continuous:   inv.Bool("continuous"),
				CreateTarget: inv.Bool("create-target"),
				Filter:       inv.String("filter"),
			})
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			return Message{Text: fmt.Sprintf("Started replication %s from %s to %s. Run \"cdb replications\" to watch it.", id, source, target)}, nil
		},
	}
}

// Replications returns the replications command.
func Replications() Command {
	return Command{
		Name:        "replications",
		Summary:     "List, inspect and cancel replications",
		Usage:       "[list | show ID | cancel ID]",
		MinArgs:     0,
		MaxArgs:     2,
		NeedsClient: true,
		// Not Destructive: "replications list" and "replications show" are
		// read-only. Only the cancel branch below is dangerous, and it calls
		// Confirm itself.
		Flags: func(fs *pflag.FlagSet) {
			fs.Bool("watch", false, "redraw every two seconds until interrupted")
		},
		Run: func(ctx context.Context, s *session.Session, inv Invocation) (Result, error) {
			sub := inv.Arg(0)
			if sub == "" {
				sub = "list"
			}
			switch sub {
			case "list":
				if inv.Bool("watch") {
					if !s.Prefs.Interactive {
						return nil, Usagef("replications", "--watch needs a terminal; drop it to print one snapshot")
					}
					return watchReplications(ctx, s)
				}
				list, err := replicate.List(ctx, s.Client)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				return replicationRows(list), nil

			case "show":
				id := inv.Arg(1)
				if id == "" {
					return nil, Usagef("replications", "usage: replications show <id>")
				}
				st, err := replicate.Show(ctx, s.Client, id)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				rows := Rows{Columns: []Column{{Title: "field"}, {Title: "value"}}}
				add := func(k, v string) {
					rows.Items = append(rows.Items, Row{Cells: []string{k, v}, JSON: jsonObject("field", k, "value", v)})
				}
				add("doc id", st.DocID)
				add("job id", st.ID)
				add("source", st.Source)
				add("target", st.Target)
				add("state", st.State)
				add("node", st.Node)
				add("errors", strconv.FormatInt(st.ErrorCount, 10))
				add("last updated", st.LastUpdated)
				if st.Error != "" {
					add("error", st.Error)
				}
				if len(st.Info) > 0 {
					add("info", string(st.Info))
				}
				return rows, nil

			case "cancel":
				id := inv.Arg(1)
				if id == "" {
					return nil, Usagef("replications", "usage: replications cancel <id>")
				}
				if err := Confirm(s, fmt.Sprintf("Cancel replication %q?", id)); err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				if err := replicate.Cancel(ctx, s.Client, id); err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				return Message{Text: fmt.Sprintf("Cancelled replication %q.", id)}, nil

			default:
				return nil, Usagef("replications", "unknown subcommand %q; expected list, show or cancel", sub)
			}
		},
	}
}

func replicationRows(list []replicate.Status) Rows {
	rows := Rows{Columns: []Column{
		{Title: "id"}, {Title: "state"}, {Title: "source"}, {Title: "target"},
		{Title: "errors", Align: AlignRight}, {Title: "updated"},
	}}
	for _, st := range list {
		rows.Items = append(rows.Items, Row{
			Cells: []string{st.DocID, st.State, st.Source, st.Target, strconv.FormatInt(st.ErrorCount, 10), st.LastUpdated},
			JSON:  mustJSON(st),
		})
	}
	if len(rows.Items) == 0 {
		rows.Hint = "no replications are scheduled"
	}
	return rows
}

// watchReplications redraws the list until the context is cancelled, then
// returns the final snapshot for the renderer.
func watchReplications(ctx context.Context, s *session.Session) (Result, error) {
	ticker := time.NewTicker(watchInterval)
	defer ticker.Stop()
	var last Rows
	for {
		list, err := replicate.List(ctx, s.Client)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		last = replicationRows(list)
		// ESC[H moves the cursor home; ESC[2J erases the display.
		fmt.Fprint(s.Stdout, "\x1b[H\x1b[2J")
		for _, item := range last.Items {
			fmt.Fprintf(s.Stdout, "%-24s %-12s %-6s %s\n", item.Cells[0], item.Cells[1], item.Cells[4], item.Cells[5])
		}
		if len(last.Items) == 0 {
			fmt.Fprintln(s.Stdout, "no replications are scheduled")
		}
		fmt.Fprintln(s.Stdout, "\nwatching; press Ctrl-C to stop")
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return Empty{}, nil
		case <-ticker.C:
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Point `cp` at `replicate.Create`**

In `internal/command/database.go`, replace `copyDatabase` with:

```go
// copyDatabase starts a one-shot replication from src to dst.
func copyDatabase(ctx context.Context, s *session.Session, src, dst path.Target) (Result, error) {
	source, err := replicate.Endpoint(s.Client, s.Path(), src.Path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, Usagef("cp", "%v", err)
	}
	target, err := replicate.Endpoint(s.Client, s.Path(), dst.Path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, Usagef("cp", "%v", err)
	}
	id, err := replicate.Create(ctx, s.Client, replicate.Request{
		Source:       source,
		Target:       target,
		CreateTarget: true,
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return Message{Text: fmt.Sprintf("Started replication %s from %s to %s. Run \"cdb replications\" to watch it.", id, src.Path, dst.Path)}, nil
}
```

and add `"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/replicate"` to that file's
imports.

### Task 21: Plain-sentence error messages and exit codes

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/render/errors.go`
- Modify: `internal/cli/cli.go` (`errorText` calls `render.ErrorMessage`)
- Modify: `internal/shell/shell.go` (`errorText` calls `render.ErrorMessage`)
- Test: `internal/render/errors_test.go`
- Test: `internal/cli/exit_test.go` (add the declined-confirmation case)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `couch.Error` (`Status`, `Name`, `Reason`, `Op`, `Target`), `couch.AsError`, `couch.StatusUnreachable`, `command.UsageError`, `command.ErrDeclined`, `path.Error`.
- Produces:
  ```go
  package render
  // ErrorMessage renders an error as a plain sentence, keeping the raw status
  // and reason only when verbose is true.
  func ErrorMessage(err error, verbose bool) string
  ```

The mapping table from the spec, section 11:

| Case | Message |
|---|---|
| 401 | `Login failed for <user> at <host>. Check the password with "profiles" or "connect".` |
| 403 | `You do not have permission to <op> <target>.` |
| 404, target is a database | `Database "mydb" does not exist. "ls /" lists databases.` |
| 404, target is a document | `Document "doc1" was not found in "mydb".` |
| 409 | `Document "doc1" was changed by someone else. "cat doc1" shows the latest version.` |
| connection refused | `Could not reach localhost:5984. Is CouchDB running?` |

Create `internal/render/errors_test.go`:

```go
package render

import (
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/couch"
)

func TestErrorMessage(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name string
		err  error
		want string
	}{
		{
			name: "401",
			err:  couch.NewError(401, "unauthorized", "Name or password is incorrect.", "read", `user "admin" at localhost:5984`),
			want: `Login failed for admin at localhost:5984. Check the password with "profiles" or "connect".`,
		},
		{
			name: "403",
			err:  couch.NewError(403, "forbidden", "You are not a db or server admin.", "delete", `database "mydb"`),
			want: `You do not have permission to delete database "mydb".`,
		},
		{
			name: "404 database",
			err:  couch.NewError(404, "not_found", "Database does not exist.", "read", `database "mydb"`),
			want: `Database "mydb" does not exist. "ls /" lists databases.`,
		},
		{
			name: "404 document",
			err:  couch.NewError(404, "not_found", "missing", "read", `document "doc1" in "mydb"`),
			want: `Document "doc1" was not found in "mydb".`,
		},
		{
			name: "409",
			err:  couch.NewError(409, "conflict", "Document update conflict.", "write", `document "doc1" in "mydb"`),
			want: `Document "doc1" was changed by someone else. "cat doc1" shows the latest version.`,
		},
		{
			name: "connection refused",
			err:  couch.NewError(couch.StatusUnreachable, "connection_refused", "connection refused", "read", "server localhost:5984"),
			want: `Could not reach localhost:5984. Is CouchDB running?`,
		},
		{
			name: "412 database exists",
			err:  couch.NewError(412, "file_exists", "The database could not be created, the file already exists.", "create", `database "mydb"`),
			want: `Database "mydb" already exists.`,
		},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			if got := ErrorMessage(tc.err, false); got != tc.want {
				t.Errorf("ErrorMessage = %q,\n           want %q", got, tc.want)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestErrorMessageVerboseAddsTheRawDetail(t *testing.T) {
	err := couch.NewError(404, "not_found", "missing", "read", `document "doc1" in "mydb"`)
	got := ErrorMessage(err, true)
	if !strings.Contains(got, "404") || !strings.Contains(got, "not_found") || !strings.Contains(got, "missing") {
		t.Errorf("verbose message %q is missing the raw status, name or reason", got)
	}
}

func TestErrorMessagePassesThroughOtherErrors(t *testing.T) {
	if got := ErrorMessage(errors.New("something broke"), false); got != "something broke" {
		t.Errorf("ErrorMessage = %q", got)
	}
	if got := ErrorMessage(nil, false); got != "" {
		t.Errorf("ErrorMessage(nil) = %q, want empty", got)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `internal/render/errors.go`**

```go
package render

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"

	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/couch"
)

// ErrorMessage renders an error as a plain sentence. With verbose set, the raw
// status, error name and server reason are appended.
func ErrorMessage(err error, verbose bool) string {
	if err == nil {
		return ""
	}
	ce, ok := couch.AsError(err)
	if !ok {
		return err.Error()
	}
	msg := plainSentence(ce)
	if verbose {
		msg += fmt.Sprintf(" [status %d %s: %s]", ce.Status, ce.Name, ce.Reason)
	}
	return msg
}

// plainSentence turns a CouchDB failure into prose a non-technical operator can
// act on.
func plainSentence(e *couch.Error) string {
	kind, name, db := describeTarget(e.Target)
	switch {
	case e.Status == couch.StatusUnreachable && e.Name == "connection_refused":
		return fmt.Sprintf("Could not reach %s. Is CouchDB running?", hostFromTarget(e.Target))
	case e.Status == couch.StatusUnreachable && e.Name == "no_such_host":
		return fmt.Sprintf("Could not find the server %s. Check the URL in the profile.", hostFromTarget(e.Target))
	case e.Status == couch.StatusUnreachable && e.Name == "tls":
		return fmt.Sprintf("The TLS connection to %s failed: %s", hostFromTarget(e.Target), e.Reason)
	case e.Status == 401:
		user, host := userAndHost(e.Target)
		return fmt.Sprintf("Login failed for %s at %s. Check the password with \"profiles\" or \"connect\".", user, host)
	case e.Status == 403:
		return fmt.Sprintf("You do not have permission to %s %s.", e.Op, e.Target)
	case e.Status == 404 && kind == "database":
		return fmt.Sprintf("Database %q does not exist. \"ls /\" lists databases.", name)
	case e.Status == 404 && kind == "document":
		return fmt.Sprintf("Document %q was not found in %q.", name, db)
	case e.Status == 404 && kind == "attachment":
		return fmt.Sprintf("Attachment %q was not found on %q.", name, db)
	case e.Status == 404 && kind == "view":
		return fmt.Sprintf("View %q was not found in %q.", name, db)
	case e.Status == 404:
		return fmt.Sprintf("%s was not found.", capitalise(e.Target))
	case e.Status == 409 && kind == "document":
		return fmt.Sprintf("Document %q was changed by someone else. \"cat %s\" shows the latest version.", name, name)
	case e.Status == 409:
		return fmt.Sprintf("%s was changed by someone else. Read it again and retry.", capitalise(e.Target))
	case e.Status == 412 && kind == "database":
		return fmt.Sprintf("Database %q already exists.", name)
	case e.Status == 400:
		return fmt.Sprintf("The server rejected the request: %s", e.Reason)
	case e.Status >= 500:
		return fmt.Sprintf("The server had a problem handling this request: %s", e.Reason)
	default:
		if e.Reason != "" {
			return fmt.Sprintf("Could not %s %s: %s", e.Op, e.Target, e.Reason)
		}
		return fmt.Sprintf("Could not %s %s.", e.Op, e.Target)
	}
}

// describeTarget picks apart the target strings couch.Client builds, which take
// the forms:
//
//	database "mydb"
//	document "doc1" in "mydb"
//	attachment "photo.jpg" of "doc1" in "mydb"
//	view "by_name" in "mydb"
//	server localhost:5984
func describeTarget(target string) (kind, name, db string) {
	fields := splitQuoted(target)
	if len(fields.words) == 0 {
		return "", "", ""
	}
	kind = fields.words[0]
	if len(fields.quoted) > 0 {
		name = fields.quoted[0]
	}
	if n := len(fields.quoted); n > 0 {
		db = fields.quoted[n-1]
	}
	return kind, name, db
}

type quotedTarget struct {
	words  []string
	quoted []string
}

// splitQuoted pulls the bare words and the double-quoted strings out of a
// target description.
func splitQuoted(target string) quotedTarget {
	var out quotedTarget
	inQuote := false
	var cur strings.Builder
	for _, r := range target {
		switch {
		case r == '"':
			if inQuote {
				out.quoted = append(out.quoted, cur.String())
				cur.Reset()
			}
			inQuote = !inQuote
		case inQuote:
			cur.WriteRune(r)
		case r == ' ':
			if cur.Len() > 0 {
				out.words = append(out.words, cur.String())
				cur.Reset()
			}
		default:
			cur.WriteRune(r)
		}
	}
	if !inQuote && cur.Len() > 0 {
		out.words = append(out.words, cur.String())
	}
	return out
}

// hostFromTarget pulls the host out of `server host:port` or
// `session on host:port`, falling back to the whole target.
func hostFromTarget(target string) string {
	fields := strings.Fields(target)
	if len(fields) == 0 {
		return target
	}
	return fields[len(fields)-1]
}

// userAndHost pulls a user name and host out of `user "admin" at host:port`,
// tolerating targets that carry neither.
func userAndHost(target string) (string, string) {
	q := splitQuoted(target)
	user := "the configured user"
	if len(q.quoted) > 0 && q.quoted[0] != "" {
		user = q.quoted[0]
	}
	return user, hostFromTarget(target)
}

func capitalise(s string) string {
	if s == "" {
		return s
	}
	return strings.ToUpper(s[:1]) + s[1:]
}
```

```go
// hostOf returns the host:port of a base URL, or the URL itself if it cannot be
// parsed.
func hostOf(base string) string {
	u, err := url.Parse(base)
	if err != nil || u.Host == "" {
		return base
	}
	return u.Host
}
```

### Task 22: Release engineering — goreleaser, CI, completion scripts, and the README

**Files:**

- Test: `internal/cli/completion_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `cli.NewRoot`, `cli.BuildInfo`, `command.Default`.
- Produces: no new Go API. The build injects
  `main.version`, `main.commit` and `main.date` with `-ldflags`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing completion test**

Create `internal/cli/completion_test.go`:

```go
package cli

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/command"
	"github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/internal/session"
)

func TestCompletionScriptsAreGenerated(t *testing.T) {
	for _, shellName := range []string{"bash", "zsh", "fish", "powershell"} {
		t.Run(shellName, func(t *testing.T) {
			var out, errOut bytes.Buffer
			s := session.New(strings.NewReader(""), &out, &errOut)
			code := Execute(context.Background(), command.Default(), s, BuildInfo{}, []string{"completion", shellName})
			if code != ExitOK {
				t.Fatalf("exit code = %d (stderr: %s)", code, errOut.String())
			}
			if out.Len() < 100 {
				t.Errorf("%s completion script is %d bytes, want a real script", shellName, out.Len())
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCompletionCommandIsPresent(t *testing.T) {
	s := session.New(strings.NewReader(""), &bytes.Buffer{}, &bytes.Buffer{})
	root := NewRoot(command.Default(), s, BuildInfo{})
	found := false
	for _, c := range root.Commands() {
		if c.Name() == "completion" {
			found = true
		}
	}
	if !found {
		t.Error("the cobra tree has no completion command")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Run: `go test ./internal/cli/ -run Completion`
Expected: exactly one failure,
`FAIL TestCompletionCommandIsPresent: the cobra tree has no completion command`.

`TestCompletionScriptsAreGenerated` already passes: it goes through `Execute`,
and cobra's `ExecuteC` calls `InitDefaultCompletionCmd` on the way in. The tree
`NewRoot` hands back, however, has no `completion` child, because cobra adds it
only during execution. (Verified in cobra v1.10.2: `InitDefaultCompletionCmd` is
called from `ExecuteC` in `command.go`, never at construction. Assigning
`cobra.CompletionOptions{DisableDefaultCmd: false, DisableDescriptions: false}`
would set both fields to their zero values and change nothing.)

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the completion command at construction time**

In `internal/cli/cli.go`'s `NewRoot`, after the `for _, c := range reg.All()`
loop that adds the subcommands and immediately before `return root`, add:

```go
	// Cobra normally adds this during ExecuteC. Adding it here as well means
	// the tree NewRoot returns is the same tree Execute runs, which is what
	// the completion tests inspect. InitDefaultCompletionCmd is a no-op when
	// the command already exists, so calling it twice is safe. It must come
	// after the subcommand loop: cobra skips it for a command with no
	// subcommands.
	root.InitDefaultCompletionCmd()
```

Cobra registers `cdb completion bash|zsh|fish|powershell` and writes the script
to the command's out stream, which `NewRoot` already points at `s.Stdout`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Run: `go test ./internal/cli/ -run Completion -v`
Expected: PASS for all four shells.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `.goreleaser.yaml`**

```yaml
version: 2

project_name: cdb

before:
  hooks:
    - go mod tidy

builds:
  # macOS needs cgo for the Keychain credential backend. Verified: with
  # CGO_ENABLED=0 the keychain backend disappears from
  # keyring.AvailableBackends() and secrets fall back to an encrypted file.
  # This build runs on a macOS runner, which can produce both arm64 and amd64.
  - id: cdb-darwin
    main: ./cmd/cdb
    binary: cdb
    env:
      - CGO_ENABLED=1
    goos: [darwin]
    goarch: [amd64, arm64]
    ldflags:
      - -s -w
      - -X main.version={{.Version}}
      - -X main.commit={{.Commit}}
      - -X main.date={{.Date}}

  # Linux (secret-service, kwallet) and Windows (wincred) backends are pure Go,
  # so those builds stay static.
  - id: cdb-static
    main: ./cmd/cdb
    binary: cdb
    env:
      - CGO_ENABLED=0
    goos: [linux, windows]
    goarch: [amd64, arm64]
    ldflags:
      - -s -w
      - -X main.version={{.Version}}
      - -X main.commit={{.Commit}}
      - -X main.date={{.Date}}

archives:
  - id: default
    ids: [cdb-darwin, cdb-static]
    name_template: "cdb_{{ .Version }}_{{ .Os }}_{{ .Arch }}"
    formats: [tar.gz]
    format_overrides:
      - goos: windows
        formats: [zip]
    files:
      - README.md
      - LICENSE*

checksum:
  name_template: "checksums.txt"
  algorithm: sha256

changelog:
  sort: asc
  filters:
    exclude:
      - "^docs:"
      - "^test:"
      - "^chore:"

homebrew_casks:
  - name: cdb
    repository:
      owner: sriannamalai
      name: homebrew-tap
    homepage: "https://github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI"
    description: "Interactive CouchDB command line client"
    license: "Apache-2.0"
    hooks:
      post:
        install: |
          if system_command("/usr/bin/xattr", args: ["-h"]).exit_status == 0
            system_command "/usr/bin/xattr", args: ["-dr", "com.apple.quarantine", "#{staged_path}/cdb"]
          end

scoops:
  - name: cdb
    repository:
      owner: sriannamalai
      name: scoop-bucket
    homepage: "https://github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI"
    description: "Interactive CouchDB command line client"
    license: "Apache-2.0"

release:
  draft: false
  prerelease: auto
```

This config is validated against goreleaser **v2.18.1**: `goreleaser check`
reports it clean, and `goreleaser build --snapshot --clean --single-target`
produces a working binary that prints its injected version, commit and date.
Note the v2.18 schema: `brews` is deprecated in favour of `homebrew_casks`
(which no longer takes a `binary` key), `archives[].builds` is now
`archives[].ids`, and `archives[].format` is now `archives[].formats` (a list).

```yaml
name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    services:
      couchdb:
        image: couchdb:3.5
        env:
          COUCHDB_USER: admin
          COUCHDB_PASSWORD: password
        ports:
          - 5984:5984
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "curl -fsS http://localhost:5984/_up || exit 1"
          --health-interval 5s
          --health-timeout 5s
          --health-retries 20
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-go@v5
        with:
          go-version: "1.27"
          cache: true

      - name: Check formatting
        run: test -z "$(gofmt -l .)" || { gofmt -l .; exit 1; }

      - name: Vet
        run: go vet ./...

      - name: Create system databases
        run: |
          curl -fsS -X PUT http://admin:password@localhost:5984/_users
          curl -fsS -X PUT http://admin:password@localhost:5984/_replicator

      - name: Unit and integration tests
        env:
          CDB_TEST_URL: http://localhost:5984/
          CDB_TEST_USER: admin
          CDB_TEST_PASSWORD: password
          CDB_KEYRING_BACKEND: file
        run: go test ./... -race -count=1

      - name: End-to-end smoke test
        env:
          CDB_URL: http://localhost:5984
          CDB_USER: admin
          CDB_PASSWORD: password
        run: |
          go build -o ./cdb ./cmd/cdb
          ./cdb version
          ./cdb mkdir /e2e
          echo '{"name":"alice"}' | ./cdb put /e2e/alice
          ./cdb cat /e2e/alice
          ./cdb ls /e2e
          ./cdb find /e2e '{"name":"alice"}'
          printf 'attachment body' > /tmp/e2e.txt
          ./cdb attach /e2e/alice /tmp/e2e.txt
          ./cdb fetch /e2e/alice/e2e.txt /tmp/e2e.out
          cmp /tmp/e2e.txt /tmp/e2e.out
          ./cdb backup /e2e /tmp/e2e.cdb.gz
          ./cdb restore /tmp/e2e.cdb.gz /e2e_copy --create
          ./cdb info /e2e_copy
          ./cdb rmdir /e2e --yes
          ./cdb rmdir /e2e_copy --yes

  build-matrix:
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-latest, macos-latest, windows-latest]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-go@v5
        with:
          go-version: "1.27"
          cache: true
      - run: go build ./...
      - run: go test ./internal/path/ ./internal/shell/ ./internal/config/ -count=1
        env:
          CDB_KEYRING_BACKEND: file
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `.github/workflows/release.yml`**

```yaml
name: Release

on:
  push:
    tags: ["v*"]

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  goreleaser:
    # macOS is required: the darwin build needs cgo for the Keychain backend,
    # and a macOS runner can also cross-compile the static linux and windows
    # binaries with CGO_ENABLED=0.
    runs-on: macos-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
      - uses: actions/setup-go@v5
        with:
          go-version: "1.27"
          cache: true
      - uses: goreleaser/goreleaser-action@v6
        with:
          distribution: goreleaser
          version: "~> v2"
          args: release --clean
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
          # A personal access token with write access to the tap and bucket
          # repositories.
          HOMEBREW_TAP_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.TAP_GITHUB_TOKEN }}
          SCOOP_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.TAP_GITHUB_TOKEN }}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write `README.md`**

````markdown
# cdb

`cdb` is a single-binary command line client for Apache CouchDB 3.2 through 3.5.
It is an interactive shell with a filesystem metaphor and a set of one-shot
subcommands for scripts.

## Install

Homebrew:

```
brew install sriannamalai/tap/cdb
```

Scoop:

```
scoop bucket add sriannamalai https://github.com/sriannamalai/scoop-bucket
scoop install cdb
```

From source (Go 1.27 or newer):

```
go install github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/cmd/cdb@latest
```

Or download a binary from the [releases page](https://github.com/sriannamalai/CDB.CLI/releases)
and verify it against `checksums.txt`.

## Quickstart

Start the shell and connect. With no saved profile, `cdb` asks for the URL,
authentication kind, user name and password, and offers to save the profile.
The password goes into the OS keychain, never into the config file.

```
$ cdb
cdb> connect http://localhost:5984
Connected to CouchDB 3.5.2 at localhost:5984 as admin.
admin@localhost:5984:/> ls
┌───────────┬──────┬──────────┬─────────────┐
│ NAME      │ DOCS │     SIZE │ PARTITIONED │
├───────────┼──────┼──────────┼─────────────┤
│ movies    │ 4211 │ 12.4 MB  │ false       │
│ _users    │    1 │ 16.3 KB  │ false       │
└───────────┴──────┴──────────┴─────────────┘
admin@localhost:5984:/> cd movies
admin@localhost:5984:/movies> ls --limit 3 --fields title,year
admin@localhost:5984:/movies> cat tt0111161
admin@localhost:5984:/movies> find '{"year":{"$gt":2000}}' --fields title,year --limit 5
admin@localhost:5984:/movies> cat tt0111161 | .title
"The Shawshank Redemption"
```

To save a connection you opened by URL, add `--save` (and `--as <name>` to
choose the profile name): `connect http://localhost:5984 --save --as local`.

Press Tab at any point to complete command names, flags, database names,
document ids, view names, and document field names sampled from the database.
`history` lists the lines you have run; repeated and unparseable lines are not
recorded. Ctrl-C abandons the running command and returns to the prompt; Ctrl-D
exits.

## One-shot use

Every shell command is also a subcommand, so `cdb` works in pipelines. When
stdout is not a terminal the output is compact JSON, one document per line.

```
cdb ls / --json | jq -r '.name'
cdb find movies '{"year":2001}' --fields title | jq -r '.title'
echo '{"title":"Amélie","year":2001}' | cdb put movies/tt0211915
cdb backup movies movies.cdb.gz
cdb restore movies.cdb.gz movies_restored --create
cdb replicate movies https://backup.example.com/movies --continuous
cdb replications
```

Exit codes: `0` success, `1` command error, `2` usage error, `3` connection or
authentication error, `130` interrupted.

## Configuration

`$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/cdb/config.toml`, which is `~/.config/cdb/config.toml` on
macOS and Linux and `%APPDATA%\cdb\config.toml` on Windows:

```toml
default = "local"

[profiles.local]
url = "http://localhost:5984"
auth = "session"          # session | jwt | none
username = "admin"
insecure_tls = false
ca_file = ""

[output]
format = "table"          # table | json
color = "auto"            # auto | always | never
pager = "auto"

[shell]
keymap = "emacs"          # emacs | vi
```

Secrets live in the OS keychain under service `cdb`, keyed by profile name.
They are never written to the config file and never accepted as flags.

For scripts and CI, these environment variables override the file and skip the
keychain entirely: `CDB_PROFILE`, `CDB_URL`, `CDB_USER`, `CDB_PASSWORD`,
`CDB_TOKEN`, `CDB_INSECURE_TLS`.

## Shell completion

```
cdb completion bash   > /etc/bash_completion.d/cdb
cdb completion zsh    > "${fpath[1]}/_cdb"
cdb completion fish   > ~/.config/fish/completions/cdb.fish
cdb completion powershell | Out-String | Invoke-Expression
```

## Commands

| Command | What it does |
|---|---|
| `connect`, `profiles`, `session` | Connect, manage saved profiles, show who you are |
| `cd`, `pwd`, `ls`, `info` | Move around and list databases, documents, views |
| `cat`, `put`, `rm`, `edit` | Read and write documents |
| `find`, `query` | Mango queries and map/reduce views |
| `mkdir`, `rmdir`, `cp` | Create and delete databases, copy documents |
| `attach`, `fetch` | Upload and download attachments, streamed |
| `conflicts`, `resolve` | Find and resolve conflicting revisions |
| `backup`, `restore` | Attachment-safe dump and load, resumable |
| `replicate`, `replications` | Start and watch replications |
| `help`, `history`, `clear`, `exit` | Shell only |

Destructive commands (`rm`, `rmdir`, `resolve`, `replications cancel`) ask
before acting. Pass `--yes` to skip the prompt in scripts. `rmdir` also asks you
to retype the database name.

`backup` writes a snapshot of the live documents and their attachments. It does
not carry tombstones, so a restored database does not know which documents were
deleted in the source; use replication, not backup/restore, to keep two
databases in step.

## Development

```
go test ./...                                    # unit tests
docker run -d --name cdb-test -e COUCHDB_USER=admin -e COUCHDB_PASSWORD=password -p 15984:5984 couchdb:3.5
curl -X PUT http://admin:password@localhost:15984/_users
curl -X PUT http://admin:password@localhost:15984/_replicator
CDB_TEST_URL=http://localhost:15984/ go test ./... # plus integration tests
```

## License

Apache-2.0.
````


```bash
CDB_TEST_URL=http://localhost:15984/ CDB_TEST_USER=admin CDB_TEST_PASSWORD=password go test ./internal/couch/ -run Integration -v
```

---

## Self-Review

Run before declaring the plan finished. This is a checklist for the plan
author, not a step for an implementer.

**1. Spec coverage.** Every spec section maps to at least one task:

| Spec section | Task |
|---|---|
| 2 Decisions (Go, module, binary, two modes, auth, 3.2–3.5) | Global Constraints, 1, 2, 6 |
| 3 Stack | Global Constraints (exact versions), 2, 5, 7, 15 |
| 4.1 Package layout | File Structure; every task creates only files listed there |
| 4.2 Command registry | 4 |
| 4.3 Session | 3 |
| 5.1 Paths, including encoded database names and partitions | 1 |
| 5.2 Prompt | 3 |
| 6.1 connect, profiles, session | 8 |
| 6.2 cd, pwd, ls, info | 4 (pwd), 9 |
| 6.3 find (with guided builder), query | 11 |
| 6.4 cat, edit, put, rm, cp, attach, fetch, conflicts, resolve | 10, 12, 13, 14 |
| 6.5 mkdir, rmdir | 12 |
| 6.6 backup, restore, replicate, replications | 17, 18, 19, 20 |
| 6.7 help, history (deduplicated, unparseable lines dropped), clear, exit, gojq filters | 15 |
| 7 Shell behaviour (readline, history, completion, Ctrl-C/D, multi-line) | 15, 16 |
| 8 Configuration and credentials | 7 |
| 9 Backup and restore format | 17, 18, 19 |
| 10 Client layer | 2, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 18, 20 |
| 11 Rendering and error mapping, exit codes | 5, 6, 21 |
| 12 Testing | Every task; `couchtest` in 2; integration gate in 2 and 22 |
| 13 Distribution | 22 |
| 14 Out of scope | Nothing in this plan builds a TUI, `tail`, cluster setup, `_config`, user management, `_active_tasks`, compaction, proxy auth, Cloudant IAM, search, 2.x support, multi-stage pipelines, or a plugin system |

**Known spec deviations, each argued from a verified fact:**

1. **`backup` spills attachments to a temp file (Task 18) before writing the
   `att` record.** The spec implies the `length` is known before the bytes are
   written. CouchDB's reported length is
   the compressed size and a streamed GET has no `Content-Length`, so the exact
   count has to be measured locally. The on-disk format is unchanged.
2. **`restore` inlines attachments under 4 MiB as base64.** The spec says to
   upload attachments after the bulk write. Verified: that bumps the revision,
   defeating the point of `new_edits=false`. Inlining preserves it. Larger
   attachments still use the spec's path, and the command says which documents
   changed revision.
3. **`internal/command` tests run against an `httptest` stub, not Kivik's memory
   driver.** The spec's section 12 names the memory driver; it cannot run
   `Query`, `Changes`, attachments or `new_edits=false`, and its `AllDocs`
   ignores every option. The stub covers all of them and lets the tests assert
   that `skip` is never sent.
4. **`adrg/xdg` is not used.** It resolves macOS config to
   `~/Library/Application Support`, which contradicts spec section 8.
   `internal/config/paths.go` implements the documented rule directly.

**2. Placeholder scan.** No task contains "TBD", "TODO", "implement later",
"add appropriate error handling", "write tests for the above", or "similar to
Task N". Every code step carries the full code. Forward references to code a
later task adds (`Client.DeleteAttachment` in Task 10, `render.ErrorMessage` in
Tasks 6 and 15, `completePath` in Task 9, `Shell.complete` in Task 15) ship with
a working stand-in **inside the same code block**, whose signature does not
change, and the later task names the exact replacement. No stand-in is printed
after the file it belongs in.

**3. Type consistency.** Checked across tasks:

- `couch.Client` methods keep one shape: `(ctx, db, …)` with a typed options
  struct, returning a typed page or raw JSON plus an error. `AllDocsOptions`,
  `FindOptions`, `ViewOptions`, `GetOptions` are each defined once.
- `couch.DocRow` is defined once (Task 9) and reused by `Query` (Task 11) and
  `conflicts` (Task 14).
- `allDocsBody` and `revFromValue` are defined once (Task 9) and reused by
  `Query` (Task 11).
- `writeResult` and `doDecode` are defined once (Task 10) and reused by
  attachments (Task 13).
- `docPath` and `docTarget` are defined once (Task 10) and reused by
  attachments (Task 13).
- `command.Result` variants (`Empty`, `Message`, `Document`, `Rows`, `Stream`,
  `Raw`) and `command.Row.Cells`/`Row.JSON` are used with the same field names
  in every command and in both renderers.
- `command.Command.CheckArgsErr` is the single argument validator, called by
  `cli.newSubcommand`, `shell.RunLine` and the tests' `invoke` helper.
- Helpers defined once and reused: `jsonObject` (Task 4, `result.go` — every
  `Rows` built from plain strings goes through it, so `--json` keys are
  lower-case throughout), `mustJSON`, `fieldString`, `humanBytes`,
  `completePath` (Task 9, `navigate.go`), `summarise` (Task 11, `query.go`),
  `closeAfterRead` (Task 13, `attachment.go`), `contains` (Task 14,
  `conflict.go`).
- `session.Prefs` field names (`Format`, `Color`, `Pager`, `Keymap`, `Yes`,
  `Verbose`, `Interactive`) are identical in `render.OptionsFor`,
  `cli.applyGlobalFlags`, `command.Confirm` and `cmd/cdb/main.go`.
  `Prefs.Interactive` has exactly two writers: `cli.Execute` (Task 6, from
  `render.IsTerminal` plus `render.IsTerminalReader`) and `runShell`/`Shell.Run`
  (Task 15, always true). No command sets it.
- `session.Session` exposes stdin only through `Stdin()`, `SetStdin` and
  `Reader()` (Task 3). Every prompt — `Confirm`, `ConfirmPhrase`,
  `promptForProfile`, `readSecret`, `buildSelector`, `chooseRevision` — reads
  from `s.Reader()`, never from a `bufio.Reader` of its own, and every test
  scripts stdin with `s.SetStdin(...)`.
- `command.Registry.Replace` (Task 4) has one caller: `shell.initHistory`
  (Task 15), which swaps `HistoryFrom(nil)` for a history command bound to the
  shell's live source.
- `couch.DatabaseInfo` (Task 9) and `replicate.Status` (Task 20) carry
  lower-case `json` tags, because both are marshalled straight into
  `command.Row.JSON`.
- The `errorText(err error, verbose bool) string` signature is the same in
  `internal/cli` and `internal/shell`, so Task 21 swaps only the body.
- Test helpers `connected(t, srv)` and `invoke(t, cmd, s, argv...)` are defined
  once, in `internal/command/navigate_test.go` (Task 9), and used by every later
  command test; `newTestClient(t, srv)` is defined once in
  `internal/couch/database_test.go` (Task 9) and used by every later client
  test. Tasks 9 through 20 must not redefine them.
